B u s i n e s s O v e r v i e w (continued)                                  業務概覽（續）
I. ENVIRONMENTAL ENERGY                                                      一. 環保能源
     Environmental energy is the largest core                                   環保能源為本集團旗下最具
     business sector with highest contribution of                               規 模、業 務 佔 比 最 高 的 核 心
     the Group. This sector encompasses waste-                                  業 務 板 塊，業 務 涵 蓋 垃 圾 發
     to-energy, food and kitchen waste treatment,                               電、餐廚及廚餘垃圾處理、滲
     leachate treatment, fly ash treatment, methane-                            濾液處理、飛灰處理、沼氣發
     to-energy, sludge treatment and disposal,                                  電、污泥處理處置、建築裝潢
     construction and decoration waste treatment,                               垃圾處理、環保產業園開發；
     development of environmental protection                                    城市綜合服務、垃圾分類、資
     industrial parks, integrated urban services,                               源 化 處 置 和 再 生 資 源，以 及
     waste sorting, resource utilisation and recycling,                         環 保 領 域 技 術 諮 詢、工 程 設
     as well as technical consultancy and engineering                           計等。回顧期內，環保能源持
     design relating to environmental protection.                               續 聚 焦 固 廢 業 務 領 域、完 善
     During the period under review, environmental                              垃 圾 發 電 產 業 鏈 佈 局，鞏 固
     energy continued to focus on the solid waste                               行業龍頭地位。
     business area, improving the layout of the
     waste-to-energy business chain, and solidifying

企業管治
COMPLIANCE WITH THE CG CODE                             遵守《企業管治守則》
The Group upholds the management principle              本 集 團 奉 行「以 人 為 本、求 真 務
of “People-oriented, Pragmatism, Creativity and         實、開 拓 創 新、規 範 管 理」的 管 理
Systematic Management”. The Board firmly believes       理念。董事會深信優質、全面及高
that high-quality, comprehensive and high-level         水 平 企 業 管 治 是 本 集 團 的 基 石，
corporate governance is the cornerstone of the          能 為 本 集 團 締 造 長 遠 價 值，對 本
Group, which can create long-term value for the         集 團 之 可 持 續 發 展 至 關 重 要，亦
Group and is vital to the sustainable development       能 維 護 股 東 及 其 他 持 份 者，包 括
of the Group. It can also safeguard the interests       但 不 限 於 客 戶、供 應 商、僱 員 及
of the Shareholders and other stakeholders of the       一 般 公 眾 的 利 益。董 事 會 及 管 理
Company, including but not limited to customers,        層竭力維持高水平的企業管治標
suppliers, employees and the general public. The        準，並 透 過 一 系 列 的 規 章 制 度 持
Board and the management strive to maintain high-       續 強 化 企 業 管 治、內 部 監 控 和 風
level corporate governance standards, and continue      險 管 理 系 統，積 極 提 高 透 明 度 和
to strengthen corporate governance, internal control    問 責 性。本 公 司 嚴 格 遵 守 營 運 地
and risk management systems through a series of         的 管 治 法 律 及 規 例，及 遵 守 監 管
rules, regulations and policies, and actively improve   機構發佈的適用指引及規則。
transparency and accountability. The Company
strictly complies with the laws and regulations of
the places where it operates, and complies with
the applicable guidelines and rules issued by the
regulatory authorities.

duly adopted by the Board as the code on corporate      治 守 則 》」）作 為 本 公 司 的 企 業 管

17. FAIR VALUE AND FAIR VALUE                            17. 金融工具之公允值及公允值
    HIERARCHY OF FINANCIAL                                   架構級別（續）
    INSTRUMENTS (continued)
    The Group’s finance management department              本集團之財務管理部門專責
    is responsible for determining the policies and        釐定金融工具公允值計量之
    procedures for the fair value measurement of           政 策 及 程 序。於 各 個 報 告 日
    financial instruments. At each reporting date, the     期，財 務 管 理 部 門 會 分 析 金
    finance management department analyses the             融 工 具 之 價 值 變 動，並 釐 定
    movements in the values of financial instruments       估值所應用之主要輸入數
    and determines the major inputs applied in             據。估 值 由 財 務 總 監 審 閱 及
    the valuation. The valuation is reviewed and           審 批。審 核 委 員 會 每 年 討 論
    approved by the chief financial officer. The           估 值 過 程 及 結 果 兩 次，以 便
    valuation process and results are discussed with       作出中期及年度財務報告。
    the audit committee twice a year for interim and
    annual financial reporting.

    The fair values of the financial assets and            財務資產及負債之公允值以
    liabilities are included at the amount at which        該工具於自願交易方（而非強
    the instrument could be exchanged in a current         迫或清盤出售）當前交易下之
    transaction between willing parties, other than        可 交 易 金 額 入 賬。下 列 方 法
    in a forced or liquidation sale. The following         及假設乃用於估計公允值：
    methods and assumptions were used to
    estimate the fair values:

    The fair values of the non-current portion of          其 他 應 收 款 項、按 金 及 預 付
    other receivables, deposits and prepayments,           款項、融資租賃應收款項、其
    finance lease receivables, other payables,             他應付款項以及計息借貸之
    and interest-bearing borrowings have been              非即期部份之公允值乃按具
    calculated by discounting the expected future          有 類 似 條 款、信 貸 風 險 及 剩
    cash flows using rates currently available for         餘年期之工具之現行適用利
    instruments with similar terms, credit risk and        率，貼 現 預 期 未 來 現 金 流 量

SCOPE OF REVIEW                                          審閱範圍
We conducted our review in accordance with Hong          我們已根據香港會計師公會頒佈
Kong Standard on Review Engagements 2410,                之《香港審閱工作準則 》第2410號
Review of interim financial information performed by     實體之獨立核數師對中期財務資
the independent auditor of the entity , issued by the    料 的 審 閱 進 行 審 閱 工 作。審 閱 中
Hong Kong Institute of Certified Public Accountants.     期財務報告主要包括向負責財務
A review of the interim financial report consists of     及 會 計 事 務 之 人 員 作 出 查 詢，以
making enquiries, primarily of persons responsible       及 進 行 分 析 性 及 其 他 審 閱 程 序。
for financial and accounting matters, and applying       由於審閱之範圍遠較根據香港
analytical and other review procedures. A review is      審 計 準 則 進 行 審 核 之 範 圍 為 小，
substantially less in scope than an audit conducted      所以不能保證我們會知悉所有在
in accordance with Hong Kong Standards on                審 核 中 可 能 發 現 之 重 大 事 項。因
Auditing and consequently does not enable us to          此，我們不會發表任何審核意見。
obtain assurance that we would become aware
of all significant matters that might be identified in
an audit. Accordingly we do not express an audit
opinion.
